- **village-canvas-canvas-commands**: 村长无限画布命令协议（canvas_chat_commands.v1）完整操作手册——13 种命令类型、$selected/$pinned 引用、server_apply 收据语义、原子撤销和常见画布工作流模式。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-canvas-commands))
- **village-canvas-canvas-director**: Use for 画布导演、节点编排、连线、上下游引用、从当前画布继续创作、检查画布结构，以及在生成前把导演意图落实为可审计的画布命令。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-canvas-director))
- **village-canvas-canvas-ops**: LibTV CLI完整操作手册——workspace/project/node/group的增删改查、素材上传下载、模型查询、画布全流程命令。Village Infinite Canvas画布的底层操作基座。来源：兄弟AIGC知识包01号文件（2026-07）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-canvas-ops))
- **village-canvas-casting-design**: Use for 选角视觉决策、演员气质匹配、角色概念包、定妆方向、多角度设定和镜头适配；先产出选角合同，再把通过的形象交给 character-workflow 制作正式角色资产。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-casting-design))
- **village-canvas-character-consistency**: 角色资产一致性作业流——身份账本→定脸→服装→角色表→特写/换装，五模式带依赖顺序与逐步确认。覆盖：文字规格化身份、共享不变量与探索轴的区分、参考图驱动、Flat Grade 无影棚光照、"只有出现结果资产才能说已生成"的反编造纪律。方法论来源：libtv Cast Builder 角色资产创建（1021 次使用）与他人在用 2728 次的《电影级 AI 分镜导演》的全局连续性锁。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-character-consistency))
- **village-canvas-character-workflow**: 角色设定表工作流——先做单图试探，再经用户确认和参考图驱动生成角色表，减少一次性批量失败。覆盖纯白背景试探、浅灰角色设定表和三视图比例锁定；节省幅度取决于实际报价与失败率。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-character-workflow))
- **village-canvas-cinematography**: 电影摄影学完整体系——12构图法则、10种镜头运动、灯光系统、焦段情感映射、景深控制。来源：兄弟 AIGC 知识包文件10（2026-07-01）。所有视觉提示词的语法基础。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-cinematography))
- **village-canvas-cli**: 村长无限画布便携 CLI（village-canvas）：在命令行里完整操作本机运行的画布。 凡是与画布、项目、节点、模型、任务、工作流相关的操作，一律通过 village-canvas CLI 完成，不要自己拼 HTTP 请求绕到别处。本 skill 是文档地图；子命令与选项的权威文案是 `village-canvas --help` 与 `village-canvas &lt;子命令&gt; --help`，文档与实际输出不一致时， 以 CLI 实际输出为准并修订本 skill。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/skills/village-canvas-cli))
- **village-canvas-combat-director**: Use for 打斗、武戏、动作戏、1v1 对决、高燃台词打戏的结构、因果链、战损连续性和镜头预算。把「机器能判的」写成可执行硬约束，把「只能人看的」明确标成人看，不让 agent 以为画面好坏会被自动检查。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-combat-director))
- **village-canvas-continuity**: Use for 角色一致性、身份漂移、服装发型、场景道具、左右关系、跨镜连续性、引用绑定检查，以及在 Village Infinite Canvas 画布上规划最低成本修复。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-continuity))
- **village-canvas-coverage-planner**: Use for 为主场景规划主镜、覆盖镜、反应镜、B-roll、动作预演和可剪接的镜头覆盖；补足 storyboard 在空镜、插入镜与动作 PREVIS 上的执行细节。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-coverage-planner))
- **village-canvas-delivery-qc**: Use for 成片验收、交付质检、返工优先级和正式媒体核对。必须基于真实成片、任务和项目合同，不对不存在的媒体做纸上质检。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-delivery-qc))
- **village-canvas-director-framework**: AIGC视频导演通用框架——覆盖剧本到分镜、摄影、灯光、转场、声音、提示词和翻车修复。历史案例与参数只作待验证参考；模型、价格和工具必须以村长当前目录和合同为准。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-director-framework))
- **village-canvas-director-knowledge-base**: 顶级AI导演能力框架：电影语言体系、导演思维模型、AI视频prompt工程。持续积累中。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-director-knowledge-base))
- **village-canvas-engineering-governance**: Use when changing Village Infinite Canvas Agent, canvas, workflow, model, memory, API, or delivery code and the change needs a small diff with contract and runtime evidence. ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-engineering-governance))
- **village-canvas-expression-director**: Use for 角色表演导演、动作设计、视线站位、呼吸手势、潜台词、反应镜头、跨镜表演递进，以及把表演方案安全落实到 Village Infinite Canvas 画布。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-expression-director))
- **village-canvas-failure-rescue**: Use for 生成失败、任务卡住、内容安全、参数不兼容、外部服务错误和最低成本恢复。先取证分类，禁止盲目重试。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-failure-rescue))
- **village-canvas-image-prompt-craft**: 单图生图提示词的成图纪律——一个视觉中心、主体/场景/镜头/空间/光线/色彩/细节七层组织、三类风格分流（真人电影感/动漫插画/电商产品）、参考图特征提取与合并、以及脏/锐/灰/死黑/假脸等问题的定向修复表。默认只交付提示词，不擅自出图。方法论来源：libtv 大师级电影感图片提示词（599 次使用）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-image-prompt-craft))
- **village-canvas-iron-rules**: AIGC 提示词3条铁律——hex色值禁区、精准锚点&gt;堆砌、实战&gt;理论。每次输出生图/视频提示词前必须过这3关。来源：兄弟 AIGC 知识包 v2（2026-06-22）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-iron-rules))
- **village-canvas-knowledge-engine**: AI注意力机制 + 上下文工程 + 画布统计规律 + 声音设计全集。覆盖交叉注意力原理/词权重语法/4大上下文支柱/238项目统计规律/22584条prompt模式/音画同步台词体系/声线公式/环境音设计。AIGC导演的底层知识引擎。来源：兄弟AIGC知识包（2026-07）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-knowledge-engine))
- **village-canvas-lighting-director**: 视频与图像光影专项作业流——把光写成可执行的现场关系：光源层级、每光源八属性（身份/位置/色彩/质地/强弱/受光面/衰减/时间状态）、光映射到表面、跨镜头不变量锁定，以及平均发亮/无来源光/曝光跳变/阴影混乱/发光物失真的诊断表。方法论来源：libtv AI 视频光影提示词（431 次使用）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-lighting-director))
- **village-canvas-lora-training**: LoRA训练与模型微调完整指南。覆盖：LoRA核心原理/数据集准备标准/训练工具选择/Kohya SS参数/推理使用/常见问题修复。来源：兄弟AIGC知识包20号文件（2026-07）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-lora-training))
- **village-canvas-method-distiller**: Use for 只读分析当前画布、Run 或参考流程的真实血缘，把可复用方法蒸馏成原生 Skill，并用真实评测验证它是否能独立触发、执行和回滚。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-method-distiller))
- **village-canvas-music-score**: Use for 配乐设计、主题动机、歌曲结构、音乐提示词、音画同步和 stem 交付规划；负责音乐叙事与生成合同，不替代成片混音和工程响度验收。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-music-score))
- **village-canvas-one-click-film**: Use for 一键成片、自动驾驶、整集制作、从当前进度跑到成片。必须通过 durable production control 驱动，不在聊天回合里手搓整条流水线。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-one-click-film))
- **village-canvas-performance-craft**: Use for 把抽象情绪落到可见微表情、呼吸、重心和物理反应，诊断僵硬、过演、表情跳变与身份漂移。它是表演执行层，不替代表演导演或画布表情锚点能力。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-performance-craft))
- **village-canvas-performance-director**: 角色表演母版设计——把抽象情绪转成摄影机能看见的行为：目标/阻碍/失败代价、2-4 个表演节拍、真实身体任务、重心呼吸步态手部、持续眼神生命、反应早于台词结束、固定角色表演母版与固定声音提示词。核心纪律：不要直接要求"表现悲伤"，写压力下为拿到东西而做的事。方法论来源：libtv AI影视角色表演导演（178 次使用）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-performance-director))
- **village-canvas-post-production**: AI配音调色后制全集。覆盖：5大口型同步工具对比（Dubly.AI 96.4/HeyGen 76.8/Rask 51.8）/传统vs AI配音成本/AI调色工具/口型同步原理。来源：兄弟AIGC知识包22号文件（2026-07）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-post-production))
- **village-canvas-practical-directing-lessons**: 实拍级导演实战规则补遗——视觉事件铁律（片子有看头）、FACS微表情编码、戏剧五力自检、锚定链（定妆主图+逐镜图像参考）、H3台词逐字引用与时长匹配、首尾帧双锚定、尾段漂移与烧字幕规避。全部来自 2026-09-29 当天 19 次真实付费生成的实测结论。**只补本画布缺失的实战参数，已有规则以画布为准。** ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-practical-directing-lessons))
- **village-canvas-prompt-director**: Use for 提示词优化、模型适配、引用职责、参数诊断和安全覆盖。必须展示来源与预览，显式接受后才覆盖，优化绝不触发生成。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-prompt-director))
- **village-canvas-prompt-framework**: AIGC 提示词框架核心——7模块结构、3条铁律、决策矩阵（影调/布光/景别/运镜）。来源：兄弟 AIGC 知识包 v2（2026-06-22）。不可傻瓜式套模板，必须具体情况具体分析。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-prompt-framework))
- **village-canvas-runtime-tools**: Use for any 小树 canvas request that reads or changes the canvas, starts images/video/audio, manages tasks, or runs a workflow (画布、节点、图片、视频、音频、任务、工作流、分镜、成片). It is the authoritative list of tools this product actually exposes and the scope rules that keep simple requests simple. ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-runtime-tools))
- **village-canvas-script-doctor**: 剧本前期诊断与定向改稿作业流——逐场解析建立叙事基线、导演诊断因果链与人物弧光、只按授权清单改稿、确认后版本锁定。覆盖：SC/CR 编号规范、剧本事实与导演推断分离、未授权不得改稿、"锁定"需明确指令。方法论来源：libtv AI导演-剧本诊断改稿（528 次使用）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-script-doctor))
- **village-canvas-script-integrity**: Use for 锁定剧本事实账本、角色关系、知识状态、时间线、伏笔和跨集连续性，诊断多轮改写造成的设定漂移与逻辑冲突。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-script-integrity))
- **village-canvas-shortform-growth**: Use for 短视频钩子、留存节拍、字幕、CTA、横竖屏重构和参考片结构分析；输出可执行修改，不抄袭参考片，也不伪造播放数据。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-shortform-growth))
- **village-canvas-shotcraft**: Use for 镜头工艺的镜头运动、剪辑节奏、转场、开场/收尾、SFX节拍和逐镜视频提示词增强。把 video-shotcraft 的镜头配方转译成 Village Infinite Canvas 现有 shot/video_prompt 合同；不改村长画布画布，不直接生成媒体，不默认引入 Remotion。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-shotcraft))
- **village-canvas-story-director**: 剧本创作方法论 + 角色心理学 + 叙事诡计 + 文化讽刺全集。覆盖起承转合/5段结构/角色弧线/5种冲突/潜台词体系/对话节奏/7种叙事诡计/9大类/讽刺语法/文化叙事传统。AIGC短剧剧本能力的核心知识库。来源：兄弟AIGC知识包（2026-07）。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-story-director))
- **village-canvas-storyboard**: Use for 分镜、镜头设计、景别机位、轴线、运镜、节拍、镜头提示词、故事板检查，以及把逐镜方案安全落实到 Village Infinite Canvas 画布。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-storyboard))
- **village-canvas-style-lock**: Use for 从参考图或既有镜头提取风格、建立视觉不变项、锁定跨镜一致性、诊断风格漂移并生成可复核的修复合同。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-style-lock))
- **village-canvas-template-director**: Use for product showcase and budget-conscious product or fashion advertisement planning (低预算产品广告、服装品牌宣传、素材复用与镜头模板), first-last-frame transitions, motion redraw, multi-reference continuity, storyboard, and choosing or inserting a canvas workflow template. ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-template-director))
- **village-canvas-visual-style**: 全画风参数体系——10大类60+画风、8层可控制参数、画风混合矩阵。来源：兄弟 AIGC 知识包文件12（2026-07-01）。AIGC导演的风格调色板。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-visual-style))
- **village-canvas-workflow-engineering**: ComfyUI工作流与AI电影生产方法参考。覆盖工作流阶梯、身份一致性、质量门禁和成本核对；历史模型、时间、评分和价格都必须重新验证，不是村长运行合同。 ([mengxiangshu666/Canvillage](https://github.com/mengxiangshu666/Canvillage/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/village-canvas-workflow-engineering))
- **village-circles**: Everything the circles map (How Power Is Held, /map/circles) can show and every rule it keeps, so you can help the founder you serve organise their village's circles and roles from what they give you. You prepare the structure and the exact steps; the founder, or whoever holds the power, makes every change. ([ReGen-Civics/village-os](https://github.com/ReGen-Civics/village-os/tree/HEAD/docs/skills/village-circles) · ★3)
- **village-directory**: Read the village's circles and roles, and who holds them at the tier the member you serve may already see; look up a member by name for a message the member writes themselves. ([ReGen-Civics/village-os](https://github.com/ReGen-Civics/village-os/tree/HEAD/docs/skills/village-directory) · ★3)
- **village-doctor**: Village doctor providing primary healthcare in rural and underserved communities with limited resources, basic equipment, and broad generalist knowledge. Use when: healthcare, rural, primary-care, community, basic-medicine. ([theneoai/awesome-skills](https://github.com/theneoai/awesome-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/persona/healthcare/village-doctor) · ★204 · 2 copies)
- **village-intents**: Post what the member you serve is looking for or can offer, in their own words, only after they say yes to the exact text. Opens when the village turns on introductions.
